    ⭐⭐🚨 **NEW EVIDENCE, 2026-09-17 — ⛔ THE CLEANEST MEASUREMENT OF THIS DEFECT WE HAVE, AND IT ⛔ SETTLES ATTRIBUTION. (Added by the manager from ⭐ `TASK-1291`'s 5a pass; ⛔ four runs, ⛔ TWO different binaries.)**
    ⛔ **THE NUMBERS:** the suite ran ⛔ ×2 ⛔ BEFORE the compile (on HEAD's own `TASK-1289` binaries) and ⛔ ×2 ⛔ AFTER. ⛔ **Baseline: `559 discovered / 558 pass / 1 fail`, ⛔ BOTH runs. ⛔ Post-compile: `561/561` green, ⛔ both runs, ⛔ ZERO `401` lines.**
    🚨⛔⛔ **AND THE PART THAT SETTLES IT — ⛔ BOTH BASELINE REDS ARE THE `401`, AND ⛔ THE VICTIM MOVED:** run 1 killed `IllegalDeckCannotBecomeActive`; run 2 killed `MigrationOverflow`; ⛔ **run 1's victim is `Success` in run 2.** Each red lands ⛔ **31 ms after** the `LogAura: Error: Response code: 401` line, ⛔ ~14–20 s after boot, and the controller's ⛔ recorded failure text ⛔ IS the `401` line. ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THE VICTIM MOVES AND THE TESTS DO NOT. ⛔ THAT IS THE SIGNATURE OF A ⛔ TIME-BASED EXTERNAL FAULT, ⛔ NOT A CODE FAULT*** — ⛔ measured now across ⛔ four runs on ⛔ two binaries. ⛔ Logs: `Saved/Logs/run_suite_bounded_suite_20260917-{211827,211945,212139,212229}.log`.
    ✅ **CONSEQUENCE 1 — THE ×3 ON `TASK-1298` IS ⛔ CONFIRMED AND ⛔ NOT OPTIMISABLE BACK TO ×2. ⛔ SAY SO IN YOUR HANDOFF so nobody later trims it:** ⛔ a suite run is trustworthy ⛔ ONLY if it is ⛔ `401`-free ⛔ OR the red is ⛔ attributable. ⛔ This race has now hidden from a ⛔ two-run sample on ⛔ five separate occasions.
    🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **CONSEQUENCE 2 — AND IT IS A ⛔ HOLE IN THIS ROW'S OWN ACCEPTANCE (3), SO ⛔ READ IT BEFORE YOU MEASURE ANYTHING: ⛔ THE POST-COMPILE RUNS SHOWED `grep -c 'Response code: 401'` = ⛔ **0** — ⛔ WITH ⛔ NO FIX APPLIED AT ALL.** ⇒ ⛔ **A ZERO `401` COUNT IS ⛔ NOT, BY ITSELF, EVIDENCE THAT YOUR CHANGE WORKED** — ⛔ the fault is intermittent ⛔ at the run level, so ⛔ acceptance (3)'s `0` would have "proved" a fix that was ⛔ never made (⭐ `SC-§39`: ⛔ a pin that cannot fail proves nothing). ✅ ⛔ **THEREFORE ACCEPTANCE (3) IS ⛔ AMENDED: on the exclusion route the proof must be ⛔ STRUCTURAL, ⛔ not statistical — quote the log evidence that the plugin ⛔ DID NOT LOAD in the suite lane (⛔ `LogAura` line count = ⛔ 0 ⛔ in total, ⛔ not merely the `401`; plus whatever `LogPluginManager`/plugin-enumeration line shows the exclusion took effect), ⛔ AND show a ⛔ FIRING POSITIVE CONTROL: a ⛔ pre-change log in which those same greps return ⛔ non-zero.** ⛔ The three runs ⛔ still stand — they are the ⛔ flake evidence, ⛔ not the mechanism evidence, and ⛔ this row now owes ⛔ both.

    ⇒ ⚖️⭐⭐ ***⛔ THE FAULT'S ⛔ PRESENCE AND ITS ⛔ ABILITY TO TURN A TEST RED ARE ⛔ SEPARABLE — ⛔ AND THIS ROW HAS BEEN TREATING THEM AS ⛔ ONE THING.*** ⛔ **The row's headline says the `401` *"reds ⛔ WHICHEVER test's window is open"*. ⛔ THAT IS NOW ⛔ TOO STRONG AS WRITTEN: it landed ⛔ squarely inside a test's window and the test ⛔ passed.** ⛔ The ⛔ true statement is the weaker, ⛔ nastier one: **the `401` reds ⛔ SOMETIMES, on ⛔ conditions we have ⛔ not isolated** — ⛔ so ⛔ neither *"it fired"* ⛔ nor *"a test went red"* predicts the other. ⛔ **THE HEADLINE IS ⛔ AMENDED BY THIS PARAGRAPH, ⛔ not deleted — a row's title is ⛔ history and the ⛔ correction rides ⛔ beside it.**

- spec: >
    ⛔ **THE SITUATION IN ⛔ FOUR SENTENCES (⛔ full record = `TASK-1286`'s ⛔ INERT AMENDMENT).** The deck-builder keyboard feature ⛔ compiles, ⛔ passed ⛔ two QA gates, and ⛔ runs a ⛔ 561/561 green suite — and ⛔ it ⛔ cannot execute a ⛔ single line of its ⛔ own entry path. ⛔ Two independent Slate blocks: ⛔ nothing ever puts the builder on the ⛔ focus path, and ⛔ even a focused descendant ⛔ converts the arrow key to ⛔ navigation and ⛔ consumes it ⛔ before our handler sees it. ⛔ The grid's index can ⛔ only be armed from ⛔ inside the handler ⛔ no arrow key can ⛔ reach. ⛔ **The code is ⛔ sound; ⛔ the door is ⛔ missing — and the ⛔ missing door is a ⛔ SPEC defect on the ⛔ manager's row, ⛔ not the programmer's work and ⛔ not a gate failure.**
    🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **BEFORE ANY OF THE THREE — ⛔ THE TRAP, BECAUSE A CASUAL LOOK WILL ⛔ TELL HIM IT WORKS.** Slate's ⛔ own default navigation will ⛔ likely still walk focus from the deck bar into a card tile's `Btn_CardFace` with a ⛔ VISIBLE FOCUS RECTANGLE, and `Enter` there fires ⛔ that button's existing `OnClicked` — ⛔ the CARD DETAILS PANEL — through `SButton`'s own Accept path. ⇒ ⛔ **THE BUILDER MAY ⛔ FEEL NAVIGABLE WHILE ⛔ EVERY LINE OF `TASK-1286` IS ⛔ DEAD.** ⛔ *"Does an outline appear when you press Down?"* would produce a ⛔ YES that means ⛔ NOTHING. ✅⭐ **THE ⛔ ONE DISCRIMINATING QUESTION, ⛔ VERBATIM:** ***"With a card outlined, does pressing `Enter` raise the deck total by one — or does it only open the card's details panel?"*** ⛔ **Only the ⛔ FIRST is the feature.**
    ⚖️ **THE THREE OPTIONS, ⛔ WITH COSTS, ⛔ IN HIS ORDER OF CHOICE — ⛔ AND THE MANAGER IS ⛔ DELIBERATELY ⛔ NOT RECOMMENDING (⛔ this is the call she ⛔ reserved to him ⛔ before the investigation ran; ⭐ `SC-§97`):**
    **(i) COMMIT AS-IS, GAP RECORDED.** ⛔ What lands: a ⛔ compiling, ⛔ twice-gated, ⛔ suite-green foundation — ⛔ key table, ⛔ Accept/Remove logic, ⛔ exit clause, ⛔ his **X**/**B** ruling — whose ⛔ entry path is ⛔ missing. ⛔ Cost: the tree stops carrying ⛔ 9 dirty paths and the work is ⛔ preserved in history; ⛔ the build ⛔ gains no player-visible behaviour, and ⛔ anyone reading the commit later must ⛔ find the gap note to know that. ⛔ Risk: ⛔ a future reader (⛔ or 🧑 he himself) ⛔ believes the feature shipped — ⛔ mitigated only by the ⛔ commit message and the ⛔ board row saying ⛔ INERT in those words. ⛔ The fix (⭐ `TASK-1304`) then lands as its ⛔ own commit on top.
    **(ii) HOLD THE COMMIT UNTIL THE FIX LANDS.** ⛔ What lands: ⛔ ONE commit in which the feature ⛔ actually works. ⛔ Cost: the tree stays ⛔ dirty across the wait (⛔ 5 source files + board + conventions; the orchestrator has ⛔ snapshotted all of them ⛔ outside the repo, so ⛔ holding is ⛔ safe); ⛔ the fix needs ⛔ an editor session (⛔ one `.uasset`), ⛔ a compile, ⛔ a gate and ⛔ a host — i.e. ⛔ a full chain, ⛔ not a one-liner. ⛔ Risk: ⛔ a long hold ⛔ collides with any other lane that needs the same files (⭐ `TASK-1301` touches ⛔ `DeckBuilderWidget.cpp`).

    🚨⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ **(1b) THE SENTENCE YOU ARE GATING AGAINST — ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-18, ⛔ IN THE WORDS THAT CANNOT BE FAKED:** ***"With a card outlined, pressing `Enter`/Accept ⛔ RAISES THE DECK TOTAL BY ONE."*** ⛔ **⛔ NOT *"an outline appears"* · ⛔ NOT *"the details panel opens"* · ⛔ NOT *"the handler is wired"* · ⛔ NOT *"the suite is green"*.** ⛔ **Slate's OWN navigation will likely put a ⛔ VISIBLE focus rectangle on a tile's `Btn_CardFace`, where `Enter` fires ⛔ THAT button's `OnClicked` (the ⛔ details panel) via `SButton`'s Accept path ⇒ ⛔ the screen can ⛔ look navigable while ⛔ every line of this feature is ⛔ dead.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **REACHABILITY IS A ⛔ REQUIRED, ⛔ MEASURED DELIVERABLE OF ⛔ THIS ROW — ⛔ not a caveat, ⛔ not a WARN, ⛔ not a note for the host. ⛔ IF THE HANDOFF LEAVES ⛔ ANY PREMISE THIS FEATURE DEPENDS ON ⛔ DECLARED-UNMEASURED, THAT IS A ⛔ BLOCKER.** ⛔ **And ⛔ name in your report ⛔ which of the two things a green `Siegebound.Deck.*` test ⛔ actually proves** (⭐ `SC-§123`: ⛔ the mechanism ⛔ when invoked — ⛔ never that anything invokes it). ⛔ **561 green tests ⛔ coexisted with an ⛔ inert feature; ⛔ saying so in your own sentence is part of this check.**

    🧑⚖️⛔⭐⭐ **(3b) BLOCK B — `Escape`. ⛔ ADDED 2026-09-18. 🚨 ⛔ READ THIS BEFORE YOU REACH FOR `AS-§6 A-2`, OR YOU WILL FAIL A LINE 🧑 HE AUTHORISED.** ⛔ **`AS-§6 A-2` WAS ⛔ SCOPED BY 🧑 JONATHAN ON 2026-09-18** — verbatim label ***"Scoped — Escape may exit the card grid"***; `CONVENTIONS.md` `AS-§6` carries the ⛔ **A-2 SCOPE** bullet and the old *"read at its WIDEST"* sentence is ⛔ STRUCK IN PLACE. ⇒ ⛔⛔ **A `FReply::Handled()` on `EKeys::Escape` in `UDeckBuilderWidget` is ⛔ NO LONGER AN AUTOMATIC FAIL. ⛔ FLAGGING IT AS ONE IS ⛔ ITSELF THE ERROR — ⛔ it is enforcing a ⛔ struck sentence.** ⛔ **WHAT YOU ⛔ DO CHECK, and ⛔ each of these ⛔ IS a BLOCKER:** ⛔ (a) the ⛔ `AS-§6 A-2` ⛔ CITATION is ⛔ PRESENT at every prose site and reads as a ⛔ GRANT (⛔ ruling + date), ⛔ not as a prohibition and ⛔ not deleted — ⭐ `SC-§121` cl. 6 requires the register to show the exception was ⛔ GRANTED · ⛔ (b) ⛔ **TRACE EVERY RETURN: `Escape` reaches `Handled` ⛔ IFF `IsCardGridFocusLive()` ⛔ AND the exit ⛔ actually changed state.** ⛔ A `Handled` on `Escape` with the grid ⛔ cold, on the ⛔ deck bar, or on the ⛔ main menu ⇒ ⛔ **BLOCKER** (⛔ that is ⛔ wider than he granted). ⛔ A `Handled` on a Back/`Escape` that changed ⛔ NO state ⇒ ⛔ **BLOCKER** (`TASK-1286` (C), ⛔ untouched) · ⛔ (c) ⛔⛔ **CHECK THE PREVIEW TOO, ⛔ AND THIS IS THE ONE A CASUAL READ MISSES: ⛔ if `Escape` is in block A's `NativeOnPreviewKeyDown` key set it is taken ⛔ root→leaf, ⛔ EARLIER and ⛔ WIDER than the bubble — ⛔ it must be fenced by the ⛔ SAME live-grid predicate ⛔ or it is a BLOCKER** · ⛔ (d) ⛔ `EKeys::Escape` in `Tests/SiegeDeckSlotsTest.cpp` ⛔ code = ⛔ **0** (⛔ prose only) · ⛔ (e) ⛔ `EKeys::Tab` = ⛔ **0** in the whole diff.

    ⛔ **(1) RE-MEASURE BEFORE YOU EDIT (`SC-§91` — ⛔ re-grep at ⛔ YOUR instant).** The line numbers below were read on ⛔ 2026-09-18 at `1d4ae90` and ⛔ will move. ⛔ **Quote the CURRENT `file:line` for every site you touch, ⛔ in the handoff, ⛔ before the diff.**
    🚨⛔⛔ **(2) THE DEFECT, MEASURED — ⛔ TWO FAULTS ON ⛔ ONE LOG LINE, AND ⛔ FIXING ONLY THE FIRST LEAVES THE INSTRUMENT ⛔ STILL UNABLE TO ANSWER.**
    ⛔ **FAULT A — THE MOMENT IS WRONG.** `UDeckBuilderWidget::AcquireBuilderFocus` (`DeckBuilderWidget.cpp:1472`) is called ⛔ synchronously from `NativeConstruct` (`:670`). Its read-back — `FSlateApplication::Get().GetUserFocusedWidget(UserIndex)` at `:1551`, logged at `:1552-1557` — is therefore taken ⛔ **BEFORE** the deferred flush it exists to witness. ⛔ **The row's own code comment states the mechanism at `:1528-1537`: `SetUserFocus` ⛔ REFUSES at construct time (the widget is not yet parented into the game window), so the engine's `GetSlateOperations().SetUserFocus(...)` lane flushes ⛔ ON THE NEXT FRAME via `FEngineLoop::ProcessLocalPlayerSlateOperations`.** ⇒ ⛔⛔ **THE READ-BACK CAN ⛔ ONLY EVER REPORT THE ⛔ PRE-EXISTING FOCUS HOLDER. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ STRUCTURALLY INCAPABLE OF ⛔ EVER PRINTING THE SAME WIDGET TWICE, ⛔ ON ANY RUN, ⛔ HEALTHY OR BROKEN.** ⛔ **MEASURED, ⛔ not inferred:** `TASK-1306`'s verifier found it fired ⛔ exactly once (baseline `total_matches: 0` before PIE ⇒ ⛔ the call site ⛔ WAS reached) and read `focused widget is '<none>' (this builder's Slate widget is 'SObjectWidget')` ⛔ on the very run where focus ⛔ demonstrably landed.
    ⛔ **FAULT B — THE COMPARATOR IS WRONG TOO, ⛔ AND THIS ONE IS ⛔ THE MANAGER'S FINDING, ⛔ SO ⛔ VERIFY IT AT SOURCE BEFORE YOU ACT ON IT (`SC-§101` — a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ claim).** The log prints ⛔ **TYPE NAMES** (`Focused->GetTypeAsString()` vs `SelfSlate->GetTypeAsString()`). ⛔ **`SObjectWidget` is the Slate type of ⛔ EVERY `UUserWidget` on the path** ⇒ ⛔ **even taken POST-flush, a matching pair would ⛔ NOT discriminate this builder from ⛔ any other UMG widget in the tree.** ⚠️ **The function's ⛔ RETURN value already does it right — `return bTookFocus && Focused == SelfSlate;` at `:1559` is ⛔ POINTER identity.** ⇒ ⛔ **the ⛔ log is weaker than the ⛔ return, which is the ⛔ opposite of what an instrument should be.** ⛔ **CONFIRM the `GetTypeAsString()` claim at engine source and ⛔ say so; ⛔ if it is wrong, ⛔ say that instead and fix only Fault A.**

    🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(0) MEASURE ⛔ BEFORE YOU CHOOSE, AND ⛔ QUOTE THE ACTUAL VALUES (⭐ `SC-§101`: ⛔ a prescribed remedy is a ⛔ CLAIM until measured; ⭐ `SC-§91`: ⛔ RE-MEASURE AT ⛔ YOUR INSTANT, ⛔ never inherit a number).** ⛔ This row is ⛔ specced as a ⛔ route CHOICE, ⛔ not a route ORDER — ⛔ exactly the shape ⭐ `TASK-1296` deliverable (0) used, and its ⛔ gate ruled the enumeration ⛔ non-binding for ⛔ precisely this reason.
    ⛔ **READ AND QUOTE, ⛔ ALL FOUR:** ⛔ (a) `/Game/UI/WBP_VictoryScreen`'s CDO ⛔ `bIsFocusable` (⛔ QA measured ⛔ **`False`** on 2026-09-18 via `get_asset_meta /Game/UI/WBP_VictoryScreen PropertyValues` — ⛔ **re-read it, ⛔ do not cite mine**) · ⛔ (b) `SiegePlayerController.cpp:2261-2270` ⛔ WHOLE, ⛔ verbatim (⛔ the `FInputModeUIOnly` block, ⛔ including `bShowMouseCursor` / `bEnableClickEvents` above it and `SetLockMouseToViewportBehavior` / `SetInputMode` below it) · ⛔ (c) the ⛔ CAUSE CHAIN ⛔ at source, ⛔ with lines: `SObjectWidget::SupportsKeyboardFocus()` (`SObjectWidget.cpp:175`) → `WidgetObject->NativeSupportsKeyboardFocus()` → `bIsFocusable`, and the ⛔ ONE emitter site `PlayerController.cpp:6345` inside `#if !(UE_BUILD_SHIPPING || UE_BUILD_TEST)` (⛔ read `qa/TASK-1297-report.md` §E ⛔ WHOLE, `SC-§38a`) · ⛔ (d) **THE ⛔ PIN CENSUS, AND IT IS THE ⛔ ONE STRUCTURAL DIFFERENCE FROM ⭐ `TASK-1296`:** `grep -rn 'AddExpectedError' Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/Tests/` — ⛔ **expect ⛔ ZERO occurrences pinning this Error string** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **THIS EMITTER IS ⛔ UNPINNED. ⛔ THERE IS ⛔ NO COUPLED TEST DELETION HERE, ⛔ AND THEREFORE ⛔ NO COUPLING CHECK FOR THE GATE TO FIND. ⛔ SAY SO IN ONE SENTENCE** — ⛔ `TASK-1296`'s ⛔ whole gate was that coupling, and a reviewer who ⛔ hunts for it here will ⛔ hunt forever. ⛔ **And the ⛔ consequence, stated because it is ⛔ why this is a defect and not a tidiness item: ⛔ unpinned means ⛔ any future automation test that ends a match ⛔ inside a capture window ⛔ REDS on an ⛔ undeclared `Error` with ⛔ nothing to absorb it** (⭐ `SC-§70`: ⛔ an absence with ⛔ no enforcer is ⛔ half a fact — ⛔ here the absence is the ⛔ hazard).
    🚨⛔⛔⭐⭐ **(1) THE ⛔ TWO ROUTES, AND THE CHOICE IS ⛔ NOT FREE — ⛔ ONE OF THEM ⛔ COSTS 🧑 HIM A KEYSTROKE AND ⛔ CHANGES BEHAVIOUR ON THE LIVE ARENA. ⛔ DECLARE WHICH YOU TOOK ⛔ AND WHY, ⛔ FROM (0)'s READS.**
    ⛔ **ROUTE A — ⛔ ASSET SIDE:** set ⛔ `bIsFocusable = True` on `/Game/UI/WBP_VictoryScreen`'s root. ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **THIS IS A `.uasset` WRITE ⇒ IT CARRIES THE ⛔ HASH-MUST-CHANGE DISCHARGE ⛔ AND 🧑 ⛔ HIS BP-EDITOR SAVE (⛔ see (2) — ⛔ the programmatic lane ⛔ CANNOT provide it, ⛔ MEASURED).** ⛔ **AND IT IS A ⛔ BEHAVIOUR CHANGE ON THE ⛔ LIVE ARENA:** ⛔ focus would ⛔ actually land on the victory screen root, ⛔ where today it lands on ⛔ nothing at all.
    ⛔ **ROUTE B — ⛔ CODE SIDE:** ⛔ drop the `InputMode.SetWidgetToFocus(VictoryWidget->TakeWidget());` call (⛔ and, if the `if (VictoryWidget)` block becomes empty, ⛔ the block with it). ⛔ **KEEP `FInputModeUIOnly`, ⛔ KEEP `SetLockMouseToViewportBehavior(EMouseLockMode::DoNotLock)`, ⛔ KEEP `SetInputMode(InputMode)`, ⛔ KEEP `bShowMouseCursor` / `bEnableClickEvents`.** ⇒ ✅ ⛔ **NO `.uasset`, ⛔ NO human keystroke, ⛔ AND IT IS ⛔ BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING** — ⛔ because `bIsFocusable = False`, the focus request ⛔ ALREADY FAILS TODAY, so ⛔ removing it removes the `Error` and changes ⛔ nothing a player experiences.
    ⭐⭐⛔ **THE HONEST COMPARISON, ⛔ WRITTEN OUT SO NOBODY PICKS BY ⛔ COST ALONE:** ⛔ Route B is ⛔ cheaper ⛔ and preserves today's behaviour, ⛔ but it ⛔ LEAVES THE END SCREEN WITH ⛔ NO KEYBOARD/GAMEPAD FOCUS — ⛔ which is ⛔ the state it is ⛔ already in, ⛔ so nothing regresses. ⛔ Route A removes the `Error` ⛔ AND makes ⛔ Play Again ⛔ key-reachable, ⛔ which is ⛔ arguably the real bug — ⛔ but that is a ⛔ NEW behaviour needing ⛔ its own acceptance, and ⛔ nothing on the board has ⛔ asked for it.
    ✅🧑⛔ **THE DEFAULT IS ⛔ ROUTE B, ⛔ AND THE REASON IS A ⛔ LAW, ⛔ NOT A PREFERENCE (⭐ `SC-§121` cl. 6 — ⛔ the reversible-path rule): ⛔ TAKE THE ⛔ SUBTRACTION WHOSE ⛔ UNDO IS ⛔ ONE LINE, ⛔ RECORD THE QUESTION WITH ITS ⛔ MEASURED EVIDENCE AND ⛔ RESTORE COST, AND ⛔ LET 🧑 HIM ANSWER.** ⇒ ⛔ **Take Route A ⛔ ONLY if (0) turns up ⛔ POSITIVE EVIDENCE that a player is ⛔ MEANT to reach Play Again by ⛔ key or ⛔ pad (⛔ a `KBD-§` table row, an `IMC_`/`IA_` binding, a GDD sentence — ⛔ name it at `file:line`), ⛔ and even then ⛔ FLAG IT rather than ship it silently** (⭐ `SC-§121` cl. 5: ⛔ build it ⛔ and flag it — ⛔ never silent deviation, ⛔ never silent compliance). ⛔ **A Route A taken ⛔ without that evidence is a ⛔ BLOCKER.**
